definition{
  name: recoveryErrorMatches,
  parameters: [
    transition : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryTransition,
    expected : Nat
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match transition
    | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryTransition.Accepted acceptedState notificationIntent => false
    | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryTransition.Rejected reason => (match reason
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.InvalidState => (0 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.IdentityDisabled => (1 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.WrongScope => (2 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.WrongAccount => (3 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.StaleRevision => (4 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.StaleEpoch => (5 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.CurrentCredentialMismatch => (6 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.CurrentAuthorizationMismatch => (7 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.InvalidRequest => (8 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.EvidenceMismatch => (9 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.UnknownCode => (10 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.ConsumedCode => (11 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.VerifierMismatch => (12 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.ReusedCode => (13 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.InvalidReplacement => (14 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.RetentionExhausted => (15 =? expected)
      | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryError.CounterExhausted => (16 =? expected))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: recoveryStateMatches,
  parameters: [
    left : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryState,
    right : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryState
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::credentialBindingsEqual (project left binding) (project right binding)) && ((match (project left enabled)
    | Bool.false => (not (project right enabled))
    | Bool.true => (project right enabled)) && (((project left retentionCapacity) =? (project right retentionCapacity)) && ((Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::savedCodesEqual (project left slots) (project right slots)) && true)))),
  axioms: [propext]
}

definition{
  name: recoveryNotificationMatches,
  parameters: [
    left : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::RecoveryNotificationIntent,
    right : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::RecoveryNotificationIntent
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: ((Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::recoveryBytesEqual (project left scope) (project right scope)) && ((Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::recoveryBytesEqual (project left account) (project right account)) && ((Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::recoveryBytesEqual (project left credentialRef) (project right credentialRef)) && (((project left previousRevision) =? (project right previousRevision)) && (((project left revision) =? (project right revision)) && (((project left previousEpoch) =? (project right previousEpoch)) && (((project left recoveryEpoch) =? (project right recoveryEpoch)) && ((match (project left credentialsRecovered)
    | Bool.false => (not (project right credentialsRecovered))
    | Bool.true => (project right credentialsRecovered)) && true)))))))),
  axioms: [propext]
}

definition{
  name: recoveryAcceptedMatches,
  parameters: [
    transition : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryTransition,
    state : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryState,
    notificationIntent : Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::RecoveryNotificationIntent
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match transition
    | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryTransition.Rejected reason => false
    | Foundation.Sec.V1.SavedRecovery::SavedRecoveryTransition.Accepted actualState actualNotification => ((recoveryStateMatches actualState state) && ((recoveryNotificationMatches actualNotification notificationIntent) && true))),
  axioms: [propext]
}
